I can't isolate it from here.\"","Ivo appeared through a service door, carrying a waterproof kit and looking personally insulted by the water.","\"Who installed this interface?\"","\"A qualified contractor,\" the guide said.","\"A universally reassuring phrase.\"","He opened the panel. Mara watched his hand tremble once before he set his tools down. The storm had changed what he could do, not what frightened him. He worked anyway.","Cassian cleared the narrow pathway towards the stairs. A metal barrier had fallen across the entrance. He and Peta lifted it far enough for Mara to crawl beneath.","\"I'll find the manual valve,\" she said.","\"Left at the first landing,\" Peta told her. \"Don't touch the silver pipe. It carries the laboratory return.\"","The little boy pointed towards the duck as his father carried him away. \"It's not supposed to be in the fish species exhibit!\"","\"Neither am I,\" Mara said, and pulled her wet jacket tighter.","Above the stairs, a mural showed a majestic reservoir surrounded by forested land. A painted geographer held a globe while an environmentalist pointed at a distant flood. The caption claimed the project would use biotechnology to irrigate agricultural ground and absorb pollution. Mara had admired it when she arrived. Now she used the mural's frame as a handhold while an alarm turned the promise into background scenery.","She went downstairs.","The air turned cold. Vapour curled above a shallow stream pouring across the steps. Her shoes found a horizontal grating, then a platform. She followed the wall to a wheel marked EMERGENCY BYPASS and put both hands around it.","It did not move.","\"Naturally,\" she said.","She planted one foot against the wall and tried again. The wheel shifted with a harsh scrape. Water struck the grating below, then began to decrease.","A new sound reached her through the pipework: a steady electronic tone, followed by a woman's voice reciting a sentence without expression.","The north window opens at six.","Mara became still.","A second voice answered, lower. The north window opens at six.","She followed the sound past the valve. A white corridor ran beneath the greenhouse. Its walls were clean, its floor dry. An observation window looked into a room containing a chair, a workbench, and a screen. A woman sat with both hands on the bench. A cuff circled her wrist.","Mara knew that cuff's shape.","A screen beside the window displayed two columns headed HIGH STRESS CONDITION and LOW STRESS CONDITION. Under each, a line moved across a graph. A researcher had left an excerpt from a funding application open on a nearby terminal. Mara caught the words \"neurotransmitter response\" before the display changed to a map of the water network.","The woman in the chair raised one hand. Her fingers trembled, then became still. Someone behind the glass adjusted a control. The woman's lips moved. Mara could not hear what she said.","A cold dismay spread through her. The labels made a human being look like a comparison that could be repeated until the metrics pleased somebody. She had seen the same discipline applied to damaged mechanisms, and despised the resemblance here.","She took out her phone and began recording.","The woman lifted her head towards the glass. She was older than Mara had first thought, with silver running through dark hair. A mask covered her lower face. Her eyes moved slowly across the window, then settled on Mara's raised hand.","She tapped the bench twice.","A door opened behind her. Someone in a surgical gown entered, and Mara stepped back into the pipeway.","\"Unauthorized access,\" a speaker said overhead.","Mara ran.","The return corridor had filled with mist. Her phone skidded against her palm. She caught a handrail, turned at a junction, and found the way blocked by a gate that had been open a moment earlier.","Beyond it stood a man with a key.","\"Put your phone down.\"","Mara looked at the gate's hinges. The lower one had been repaired with a modern screw in an older fitting. Its head was the wrong size.","\"I'd prefer to discuss your installation,\" she said.","He reached for the latch. She drove the flat end of her tool into the hinge and twisted. The gate dropped two centimetres with a violent clang. Its edge struck his shin. She ducked beneath it and ran past him while he gasped for air.","The next door opened onto an agricultural room filled with trays of green shoots. Solar lamps blazed overhead. Water ran through channels beneath the roots. A diagram on the wall described an artificial ecosystem designed to absorb waste from the public greenhouse.","Another door bore the words NEUROBIOLOGY UNIT.","Mara did not stop to investigate. Footsteps were gaining behind her.","She overturned a wheeled cart into the narrow passage, scattering empty sample racks. A man struck it at speed and fell. Mara snatched a laboratory coat from a hook, put it over her wet dress, and walked through the next doorway carrying a tray.","Two researchers looked up from a microscope.","\"Priority delivery,\" she said.","One pointed absently towards a cupboard. \"Put it below the filtered specimens.\"","She did. Then she kept walking, pulse hammering, until a hand caught the back of her coat.","Cassian drew her into a vacant alcove and kissed her hard enough to stop the answer she had prepared.","She caught his shirt. For one second the underground world ceased to exist.","\"You took the wrong turn,\" he whispered against her temple.","\"You have a doctorate in understatement.\"","\"And you're applying for a fellowship in risk-taking.\"","\"I thought you admired my initiative.\"","\"I admire quite a few things about you. At present, I would prefer to admire them outdoors.\"","She pressed a quick kiss to the corner of his mouth. \"A sincere incentive.\"","His answering look was not remotely sober-sided. For a heartbeat the danger acquired an unexpected upside, then a shout in the corridor reminded them that nobody had hired an entourage to applaud.","\"I was improvising.\"","\"I noticed. Your audience is following.\"","He lifted a maintenance hatch. Mara went through first. Cassian followed, closing it just as the corridor door opened.","They crouched in a tunnel scarcely wide enough for their shoulders. Warm air moved against Mara's face. Through a grille, she could see a corner of the laboratory screen.","A three-dimensional image turned above a label: CARRIER REPRODUCTION, BATCH 11. A second field read CONTACT INTERFACE: ACTIVE.","\"There was a woman,\" Mara whispered. \"With the cuff. Like the ship.\"","Cassian's jaw tightened. \"Did you get a recording?\"","She showed him the phone. The file was intact.","Outside, someone ordered a security sweep. A chair scraped across the floor.","Mara rested her head briefly against Cassian's shoulder. His hand closed around hers, steady and warm.","\"How did you get down here?\"","\"Ivo asked a question about wiring. Forcefully.\"","\"A heroic distraction.\"","\"He dislikes having his competence questioned.\"","The tunnel ended at a stand-alone air unit. Cassian tried its hatch, but the catch had jammed. The motor's continuous hum rose, then fell into a frightening silence.","\"They've stopped the fan,\" he said.","A sufficiency of air had never seemed a luxury until Mara could no longer take it for granted. She turned her light inward, found the jammed hinge, and slid a thin blade beneath it.","\"Give me your cuff link.\"","\"The left one is silver.\"","\"Materialistic nobility. The other.\"","He handed it over without another objection. She inserted it as a wedge, pushed, and felt the catch retreat. The hatch opened onto a narrower passage.","Cassian caught her wrist before she climbed through. \"Keep it.\"","\"As a romantic symbol?\"","\"As a tool. You appear to value those more highly.\"","She smiled in the dark. \"Lucky guess.\"","They moved along the tunnel on hands and knees. The space narrowed beneath a concrete beam. Mara felt her breath catch, remembered the ship's confined compartment, and forced herself to look at the pale square of light ahead.","Cassian followed close enough for his shoulder to brush her foot. \"One more turn.\"","The hatch opened into a forested courtyard below street level. Rain fell through a narrow gap in the roof. Ferns crowded the stone. Above them, a glass bridge connected two wings of the compound.","Peta stood beside a water barrel. She did not look surprised.","\"Quickly,\" she said.","She led them through a greenhouse of exotic plants, past a moist bed of orchids and a fountain built around a Greek stone fragment. A research tent occupied one corner. Its table held a map of the old quarry and a stack of funding applications.","Mara caught one heading: ADAPTIVE MEMORY ENVIRONMENT.","Peta folded the sheet and put it in her pocket.","\"My project is botany,\" she said. \"Their project isn't.\"","A guard shouted from the bridge.","Peta pushed Mara towards a narrow door. \"The north exit. Go.\"","\"Come with us,\" Cassian said.","\"If I leave now, the people upstairs lose the only person who can stop the next overflow.\"","She turned back towards the glass bridge, her coat flying like a white wing.","Mara and Cassian ran through a courtyard gate into the service lane. Ivo waited beside a bicycle cart loaded with bags of soil. June occupied the driver's seat of a small delivery van, wearing a stranger's cap.","\"I object,\" Ivo announced, as they climbed into the back. \"To almost every component of this expedition.\"","Mara lay across the soil bags, laughing with exhausted disbelief. Cassian braced one hand above her head as the van lurched forward.","A siren began behind them.","June looked into the mirror. \"Nobody breathe like a criminal.\"","\"How does a criminal breathe?\" Mara asked.","\"Expensively. You're getting soil on my borrowed upholstery.\""],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"What does Mara’s escape through the gate chiefly demonstrate?","options":["Her attention to mismatched
